| Type | Group | Count | Use |

| `TAXONOMY` | Taxonomies and Governance (Layer C0) | 300 | Educational standards, locations, org-levels |
| `BLOCK_SUBJECT_AREA` | Taxonomies and Governance (Layer C0) | 102 | Subject areas |
| `BLOCK_RUBRIC` | Curricular Regulations (Layer C0) | 399 | Grading rubrics per subject and level |
| `RECIPE` | Structures and Recipes (Layer C1) | 857 | Exercise recipes (step structure) |
| `LIBRARY` | Structures and Recipes (Layer C1) | 24 | Exercise type libraries |
| `PATHWAY_TEMPLATE` | Curricular Planning and Pathways (Layer C1/C2) | 67 | Learning pathway templates |
| `BUNDLE_COURSE` | Curricular Planning and Pathways (Layer C1/C2) | 444 | Course bundles with curricular units |
| `BLOCK_CONTENT` | Exercises and Assessments (Layer C2) | 1491 | Content blocks: exercises and assessments |
| `PERSONA` | Agents and AI (Layer C3) | 40 | AI evaluator personalities |
| `BLOCK_INSTRUCTION` | Agents and AI (Layer C3) | 49 | Pedagogical directives |
| `BUNDLE_TOPIC` | Others | 2 | BUNDLE_TOPIC |
| `INTERACTIVE_LESSON` | Others | 667 | INTERACTIVE_LESSON |

## CLI Commands

```bash
# List all taxonomies
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog list --type TAXONOMY

# List rubrics by standard
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog list --type BLOCK_RUBRIC --root es.c0

# Show spec detail
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog show --id std.es.lomloe

# Global tree
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog tree --depth 1

# Regenerate this page
bash ce-cli.sh taxonomy catalog docs
```
